athletics_results=# \d+ athlete_summary
                                  View "public.athlete_summary"
      Column       |       Type        | Collation | Nullable | Default | Storage  | Description
-------------------+-------------------+-----------+----------+---------+----------+-------------
 athlete_name      | character varying |           |          |         | extended |
 club              | text              |           |          |         | extended |
 country           | text              |           |          |         | extended |
 result_count      | bigint            |           |          |         | plain    |
 competition_count | bigint            |           |          |         | plain    |
View definition:
 SELECT athlete_name,
    max(club::text) AS club,
    max(country::text) AS country,
    count(*) AS result_count,
    count(DISTINCT competition_id) AS competition_count
   FROM results
  GROUP BY athlete_name;

athletics_results=# SELECT
athletics_results-#     athlete_name,
athletics_results-#     event_name,
athletics_results-#     age_group,
athletics_results-#     club,
athletics_results-#     performance,
athletics_results-#     status
athletics_results-# FROM results
athletics_results-# WHERE competition_id = 27
athletics_results-# AND (
athletics_results(#     performance ILIKE 'Notes:%'
athletics_results(#     OR performance = club
athletics_results(#     OR performance ~ '^U[0-9]+$'
athletics_results(#     OR performance IN ('Open', 'Senior')
athletics_results(# )
athletics_results-# ORDER BY event_name, athlete_name;

athletics_results=# SELECT
athletics_results-#     athlete_name,
athletics_results-#     event_name,
athletics_results-#     performance,
athletics_results-#     status
athletics_results-# FROM results
athletics_results-# WHERE competition_id = 27
athletics_results-# AND performance IS NOT NULL
athletics_results-# AND performance ~ '[A-Za-z]'
athletics_results-# AND performance NOT IN (
athletics_results(#     'DNS',
athletics_results(#     'DNF',
athletics_results(#     'DQ',
athletics_results(#     'NM'
athletics_results(# )

SELECT
    competition_id,
    athlete_name,
    event_name,
COUNT(*)
FROM results
GROUP BY
     competition_id,
     athlete_name,
     event_name
 HAVING COUNT(*) > 1
 ORDER BY competition_id;
